# log.md

## 0. 文件用途

这是 Codex 执行记录文件。

每次 Codex 完成任务后，都应更新本文件。

不要删除历史记录。  
不要伪造运行结果。  
如果没有实际运行成功，不要填写虚假的统计结果。

### 2026-07-09 - 解释 B>C 仍制氢但 A 看起来小的原因

### 2026-07-09 - 解释 theta_value 未来近似成本总额的作用

- 读取规则文件：`codex_rule/core.md`、`codex_rule/longtask.md`、`codex_rule/log.md`。
- 说明 A=`theta_value` 不是无用量，而是阶段优化目标中用于代表未来成本总额的变量；模型比较的是当前阶段成本加未来近似成本。

- 读取规则文件：`codex_rule/core.md`、`codex_rule/longtask.md`、`codex_rule/log.md`。
- 确认 A=`theta_value` 是未来近似成本总额，B=`marginal_value_of_1kg_LOH` 是单位库存价值/斜率。

### 2026-07-09 - 解释 theta_value 与 marginal_value_of_1kg_LOH 的关系

- 读取规则文件：`codex_rule/core.md`、`codex_rule/longtask.md`、`codex_rule/log.md`。
- 解释 `theta_value` 是未来成本近似总量，单位为元；`marginal_value_of_1kg_LOH` 是该近似函数对站点库存的单位变化量，单位为元/kg。
- 明确判断是否值得多制/多存 1kg 氢时，应比较 `marginal_value_of_1kg_LOH` 与制氢、持有、运输等单位成本，而不是直接比较 `theta_value`。

- 读取规则文件：`codex_rule/core.md`、`codex_rule/longtask.md`、`codex_rule/log.md`。

### 2026-05-15 - RiskCap-OT full-cover-first、generalized KL 与独立 tau 修正

- 在 `generate_terminal_loh_wind_mc_preview.m` 的 RiskCap-OT final-candidate 路径中新增 full-cover-first 逻辑：每个终端状态、每个 priority mode 求解前先做容量/可达性最大流全覆盖可行性检查；全覆盖可行时强制 `u_n=0`；只有不可行时才允许 uncovered。
- 将 RiskCap-OT KL 项从 `tau * sum(y*log((y+eps)/(reference+eps)))` 改为 generalized KL：`tau * sum(y*log((y+eps)/(reference+eps)) - y + reference)`，并同步修改梯度为 `logRatio + y/(y+eps) - 1`。
- 将 `optsWind.riskCapOTTau` 从继承 `optsWind.roadTau=10` 改为独立参数 `0.2`；roadSoft 仍使用 `roadTau`。

- 主要结果：a=6 loc=5 fallback_node_count 从 VN=25 的 12 降为 VN=30/35/40 的 0；a=6 loc=6 fallback_node_count 从 VN=25 的 17 降为 VN=30/35/40 的 0。
- pClose 结果：loc5 mean 从 0.848162 降至 0.532455/0.304716/0.159442；loc6 mean 从 0.916964 降至 0.592357/0.348922/0.191208。VN=25 下 pClose=1 边数 loc5=21、loc6=22；VN=30/35/40 下均为 0。
- 解释：当前程序中 `riskFactor=pClose`，因此调高 roadDesignWindVN 不仅降低道路关闭概率，也同步降低未关闭道路的 slowdown。fallback 明显下降说明 VN=25 对道路侧偏保守。
- 后续建议：可单独评估更高 roadDesignWindVN；也可考虑把 slowdown 风险因子从 pClose 中解耦。当前仅提出建议，未接入 MSP。

- 主要结论：a=6 loc=5 的计数 fallback 节点为 2,3,4,5,6,19,21,22,23,26,27,29；a=6 loc=6 的计数 fallback 节点为 5,6,7,8,9,10,11,12,13,15,16,17,28,29,30,32,33。所有计数 fallback 节点对四站 reachability_probability 均为 0。
- 原因判断：主要是极端 road close probability 导致 road accessibility collapse。loc5 更像 pClose=1 边造成的拓扑割裂；loc6 多数节点被 pClose=1 边割裂，少数节点存在理论可通路径但路径开通概率极低，200 次 MC 中观测为 0。不是 service cost 公式本身触发。
- 后续建议：可在后续单独尝试 small/mid/large Rmax 加权 road accessibility，作为可达性平滑和鲁棒性检查；本次未实现。

### 2026-07-07 - WDRO 距离主方法改为 DA，并新增 DAC_maskedC

- 本次只修改 `terminalLoh_wdro/` 下文件，并更新 `codex_rule/log.md`；未修改 `main_msp_h2_near.m`、`run_h2_with_options.m`、`load_data_h2_near.m`、`h2_default_options.m`、`fa_h2/` 主流程文件、`data/` 原始概率表或 `data/yuanqi/near_stage_msp_input.mat`。
- 修改 `terminalLoh_wdro/src/build_wdro_distance_matrix_h2.m`：新增主距离 `DA`，定义为 `w_D*d_D + w_A*d_A`，默认权重 `w_D=0.7`、`w_A=0.3`；A 作为有限离散场景原子中的二进制可达性矩阵，不做连续扰动。
- 修改 `build_wdro_distance_matrix_h2.m`：新增扩展距离 `DAC_maskedC`，默认权重 `w_D=0.6`、`w_A=0.25`、`w_C=0.15`；C 只在两个场景共同可达的 site-node 对上比较，避免 unreachable 事件被 A 和 C 重复计量。
- 原 `DAC` 保留为 legacy/unmasked compatibility mode，输出标签为 `DAC_legacy_unmasked`，默认运行不再包含该模式。
- 修改 `terminalLoh_wdro/src/run_terminal_loh_wdro_preview_h2.m`：默认 `distance_modes={'D_only','DA','DAC_maskedC'}`，默认输出目录改为 `terminalLoh_wdro/output/stage1_single_window_DA_update/`。
- 修改 `terminalLoh_wdro/src/build_terminal_loh_wdro_from_joint_samples_h2.m`：扩展 `wdro_distance_matrix_summary.csv` 字段，新增 `d_diag_max_abs`、`d_symmetry_max_abs`、`A_binary_ok`、`A_unique_values`、`A_total_reachable_*`、`C_unreachable_*`、`C_masked_pair_count/share` 和各距离权重；`wdro_distance_matrix_summary.csv` 现在每个状态/距离模式一行，不再按 rho 重复。
- 修复 `build_rho_sensitivity_summary` 中 MATLAB `innerjoin` 自动列名导致的汇总错误，改为按排序后的状态直接比较相邻 rho。
- 新增文档 `terminalLoh_wdro/docs/README_WDRO_distance_DA.md`，说明 DA 主距离、有限离散支持集、不会产生 `A=0.3` 半可达状态、为什么 C 不进入主距离、`DAC_maskedC` 和 rho 选择。

### 2026-07-07 - 补充 WDRO 距离中道路可达但变慢的说明

- 仅修改 `terminalLoh_wdro/` 下文档、`build_wdro_distance_matrix_h2.m` 顶部代码注释，以及 `codex_rule/log.md`；未修改 MSP 主流程、原始 `data/` 文件或 TerminalLOH 接口。

- 补充说明：`A` 只能表示二进制可达性，`A=1` 为存在可行服务路径，`A=0` 为完全不可达，不允许用 `A=0.3` 表示半可达。
- 补充说明：道路因积水、拥堵、倒树、绕行、限速等仍可通行但变慢时，应保持 `A=1`，并通过更大的 `C` 表示服务成本、通行阻抗或通行时间增加。
- 补充说明：DA 主距离 `xi=(D,A)` 不使用 C；`DAC_maskedC` 只比较两个场景共同可达位置的 C，一个可达、一个不可达时差异只由 A 表达，unreachable 侧的 `Inf` 或大 M 不进入 masked C 距离。
- 本次未改变距离计算逻辑，未重跑 LP，未生成新 D/A/C 样本，未新增 CVaR，未接入 MSP。

### 2026-07-14 - W1-W3 损坏持续性 smoke test

- 本次读取并遵守 `codex_rule/core.md`、`codex_rule/longtask.md`、`codex_rule/log.md`；`core.md`、`longtask.md` 保持只读。
- 新增代码：`terminalLoh_wdro/src/run_stage2_damage_persistence_smoke_h2.m`、`evaluate_damage_persistence_mode_h2.m`、`compare_independent_persistent_damage_h2.m`、`write_damage_persistence_diagnostics_h2.m`。未修改既有 Foundation v1/v2 代码结果或 MSP 文件。
- 测试参数：`Wstep=40`、`Rmax=[30,40,50]`、`a=[4,6]`、`loc=[0,3,10]`、`W1/W2/W3` 各 1 h、`Hres=3 h`、`Nmc=200`、`rng_seed=20260715`。
- 两种模式：`independent_snapshot` 每阶段直接使用当期线路/道路 Bernoulli 抽样和当期慢行严重度，允许后续恢复；`persistent_damage` 对线路故障和道路关闭做阶段累计 OR，对慢行严重度做累计 max，不包含修复机制。
- common random numbers：同一 Rmax/a/loc/stage/scenario 的 seed、线路 uniform checksum、道路 uniform checksum、原始故障/关闭抽样数和基础 pFail/pClose 均一致；自动检查通过。
- 输出规模：`damage_persistence_by_slice.csv` 为 21600 行，等于 `2 modes * 3 Rmax * 2 a * 3 loc * 200 scenarios * 3 stages`；Hres3h summary 36 行，模式比较 18 行。
- persistent 单调性：线路故障数量递减违例 0，道路关闭数量递减违例 0，慢行严重度递减违例 0。independent 模式中有 1981 个场景出现后续线路故障数或道路关闭数下降，确认恢复行为真实存在。
- 聚合结果，阶段顺序均为 W1/W2/W3。Rmax=30：independent failed=`[2.0483,8.8983,5.0942]`、P_loss=`[890.12,1976.15,1429.97] kW`、D=`[48.5567,107.8008,78.0062] kg`；persistent failed=`[2.0483,9.3942,11.6758]`、P_loss=`[890.12,1980.59,2281.59]`、D=`[48.5567,108.0431,124.4629]`。
- Rmax=40：independent failed=`[4.7575,10.3575,6.3958]`、P_loss=`[1272.06,2455.73,1358.46] kW`、D=`[69.3919,133.9625,74.1051] kg`；persistent failed=`[4.7575,11.8900,13.9725]`、P_loss=`[1272.06,2489.85,2660.37]`、D=`[69.3919,135.8234,145.1254]`。
- Rmax=50：independent failed=`[8.7017,10.0475,6.6158]`、P_loss=`[2147.38,2305.42,1291.87] kW`、D=`[117.1413,125.7626,70.4727] kg`；persistent failed=`[8.7017,14.2733,16.0075]`、P_loss=`[2147.38,2701.15,2767.41]`、D=`[117.1413,147.3500,150.9649] kg`。
- Hres3h D_total 平均值：Rmax 30/40/50 下 independent 分别为 `234.363664/277.459564/313.376565 kg`，persistent 为 `281.062652/350.340716/415.456091 kg`，增量分别为 `46.698988/72.881152/102.079526 kg`，相对增幅 `19.93%/26.27%/32.57%`。
- 饱和诊断：persistent 跨 a/loc/scenario 聚合最大饱和率为 `0.458333`，低于大量饱和阈值 0.5，因此未判为整体大量饱和；部分高风险状态级 summary 的最大饱和率可达到 1，正式 B3 仍需保留分状态饱和诊断。
- Wstep=40 阶段区分度保留：persistent 的 D 阶段 range ratio 在 Rmax 30/40/50 下约为 `0.6099/0.5218/0.2240`，均高于 0.05 阈值；故障数、道路关闭和 P_loss 也保持阶段差异。
- 正式 B3 设计建议：在“3 h 内不考虑修复”的假设下，建议以 `persistent_damage` 为主口径，并保留 `independent_snapshot` 作为消融对照；正式 B3 尚未执行。
- 最终自动检查：14/14 必需项通过，0 失败；2 行饱和和阶段区分度为 NaN 诊断项。保护的 8 个旧输出目录和 14 个 MSP/数据文件均未变化；源调用扫描 WDRO/Gurobi、MSP、B3/path_prob 命中均为 0。

- 固定参数：`Wstep=40` 由入口配置传入 evaluator，不在阶段推进公式中硬编码；`Rmax=[30,40,50]`、`a=[4,6]`、`loc=[0,3,10]`、`Nmc=200`、`seed=20260716`，W1/W2/W3 各 1 h，Hres=3 h。
- 三种模式：`independent_snapshot` 使用阶段独立抽样并允许恢复；`persistent_independent_draws` 使用阶段独立抽样后累计 OR/max；`persistent_fixed_resistance` 每场景每组件只使用一次 W1 uniform 阈值，W1-W3 共用该阈值，首次损坏后持续，无修复。
- common random numbers 自动检查通过：三种模式共享相同基础 stage-uniform cube、seed、pFail/pClose；W1 线路、道路、P_loss、D、A/C 后果完全一致。固定抗力阈值逐场景跨 W1-W3 精确复用检查通过。
- 道路 A/C：使用 `stage1_road_edges.csv`、`stage1_site_nodes.csv` 和 Foundation 坐标；关闭边不进入最短路，慢行边成本为 `edge_length*(1+slowdown)`；A 为 4×33 二进制可达性，C 为当前最短路 `dist(n)`，没有 `baseCost+dist(n)`。不可达 C 未进入有限成本统计。
- 输出规模：`damage_persistence_v2_by_slice.csv` 32400 行，`damage_persistence_v2_Hres3h_summary.csv` 10800 行，`damage_mode_comparison_v2.csv` 54 行，`state_level_saturation_v2.csv` 162 行，`road_AC_stage_diagnostics_v2.csv` 32400 行，检查表 27 行；另输出诊断摘要、实现审计和 6 张 PNG。
- D_Hres3h 均值，Rmax=30/40/50：independent 为 `232.325051/275.307303/310.597651 kg`；persistent independent draws 为 `279.561138/350.107965/414.789206 kg`；persistent fixed resistance 为 `275.745529/337.679222/393.952122 kg`。
- 累计 OR 相对固定抗力的 D 均值增量，Rmax=30/40/50 分别为 `3.815609/12.428743/20.837084 kg`，相对 `1.38374%/3.68064%/5.28924%`；总体增量 `12.360478 kg`，相对 `3.68099%`。分状态最大有限相对增幅为 `32.4278%`（Rmax=40,a=6,loc=10）；另有低风险状态 Rmax=30,a=6,loc=10 出现 fixed=0、累计 OR=0.343671 kg 的重复机会效应。

### 2026-07-23 - task-002 Step-03D run-001 主体 B3 样本量稳定性验证

- 当前分支：`task/002-stage2b-b3-smoke`。新增 `evaluate_nominal_b3_stability_block_h2.m` 和 `run_stage3d_b3_sample_stability_h2.m`；未修改主体样本、候选池、转移矩阵、Step-03A/B/C 旧 run 或 MSP 文件。
- 主体输入为 35 个初始状态、每状态 15000 条 Monte Carlo 抽样记录。每个状态和三个种子 `20260723/20260724/20260725` 使用一个确定性随机排列，测试 `N=[500,1000,2000,5000,10000,15000]` 的嵌套前缀；每条记录权重严格为 `1/N`，权重和为 1，不再次乘 `path_probability`。
- 每条主体记录生成一个 `persistent_fixed_resistance` 场景。使用 12 个本地 MATLAB worker 完成 1575000 个正式场景，并对全部 105 个状态/种子块的 N=500 前缀复算 52500 个场景；同种子复算 105/105 一致。
- 电网为 33 节点、32 线路径向网络，使用源节点到节点的线路路径矩阵批量计算持续故障后的失负荷；道路仍按 Step-03B 当前关闭边、持续慢行和四站到 33 节点的最短路计算 A/C，没有改变物理口径。
- 只使用主体抽样记录。268 条已观察尾部路径仅按其在主体样本中的自然记录出现；外部候选追加数为 0，858 条未观察候选在主体记录中的命中数为 0。
- 相对同状态同种子 N=15000，N=500/1000/2000/5000/10000 的 p95 绝对误差：D mean=`12.4923/9.7748/5.8828/3.3914/1.9514 kg`；D q95=`50.7323/36.0036/29.4575/16.0925/7.0916 kg`；D q99=`73.3710/42.5497/27.2755/22.9114/13.0922 kg`。
- 对应 full-loss probability p95 绝对误差为 `0.0252/0.0202/0.0092/0.0050/0.002833`，A=0 share 为 `0.015970/0.009228/0.006277/0.003378/0.001618`，reachable C mean 为 `0.16945/0.15413/0.09403/0.05037/0.02870 km`，reachable C q95 为 `0.89813/0.67963/0.45014/0.21681/0.13709 km`。
- 最难收敛指标：低概率全失负荷在相对误差口径下最不稳定，因为部分状态 N=15000 参考接近 0；D q99 在绝对误差和种子差异下最慢。N=10000 时 D q99 的 p95 绝对误差仍为 `13.0922 kg`，p95 seed std 为 `16.2771 kg`。
- 项目没有现成的 B3 D/A/C 稳定性阈值；未借用 Stage2A2 路径分布阈值，也未新造门槛。`recommended_sample_size.csv` 保守建议正式 B3 使用每状态 `N=15000`，并明确标记为 diagnostic recommendation、不是 threshold acceptance。

### 2026-07-23 - task-002 Step-03E run-001 强度等级—风速映射敏感性验证

- 当前分支：`task/002-stage2b-b3-smoke`。新增 `evaluate_intensity_wind_sensitivity_block_h2.m` 和 `run_stage3e_intensity_wind_sensitivity_h2.m`；正式强度到 Vmax 映射、主体样本、1126 条候选池、W 转移矩阵、Step-03A/B/C/D 旧 run 和 MSP 文件均未修改。
- 使用 Step-03A 的 5 个代表状态：`(5,4,0)`、`(2,6,0)`、`(2,1,0)`、`(4,4,0)`、`(6,7,0)`。每状态复用 Step-03D 三个种子 `20260723/20260724/20260725` 的同一确定性排列前 2000 条主体记录，共 30000 个路径-抗力场景；四种映射共计算 120000 组后果。
- 所有模式严格共用路径、线路固定抗力和道路固定抗力。M1 的 `q~Uniform(0,1)` 在同一 B3 联合后果实现中生成，每条路径实现只有一个 q，并在 W1-W3 共享；该 q 只是等级内连续风速敏感性假设，不是正式风速分布或第三层 Monte Carlo。
- M0 使用当前固定映射 `[0,20.8,28.55,37.05,46.20,55.50] m/s`。a=2:5 的边界采用现行国家标准 `GB/T 19201-2006 热带气旋等级` 的 `[17.2,24.4]`、`[24.5,32.6]`、`[32.7,41.4]`、`[41.5,50.9] m/s`；标准主管和归口部门均为中国气象局。a=1 保持 0；a=6 因无可追溯有限上界，在 M0/M1/M2L/M2H 中均保持 55.5，未编造上界。
- M0 与 Step-03D N=2000 的 5 状态、3 种子、12 项指标逐项复算一致，最大绝对差 `4.54747350886464e-13`，小于 `1e-12` 容差。
- 15 个状态/种子块的跨块平均结果：M0/M1/M2L/M2H 的 D mean=`153.85/155.24/109.28/205.29 kg-H2`，D q95=`341.22/348.93/318.58/364.09 kg-H2`，D q99=`390.00/392.28/353.98/427.73 kg-H2`。相对 M0，M1 平均 D 变化 `+1.39 kg-H2`；M2L/M2H 分别为 `-44.57/+51.44 kg-H2`。
- M0/M1/M2L/M2H 的平均全失负荷率约为 `0.03/0.04/0.02/0.06`，平均 A=0 share=`0.06/0.06/0.03/0.10`，reachable-only C mean=`20.04/20.05/19.65/20.52 km`，W3 故障线路数=`4.49/4.55/2.89/6.52`，W3 关闭道路数=`2.33/2.40/1.29/3.80`。
- 阈值解释：相对 M0，M2H 新增 282749 个线路局部风速观测跨越 25 m/s、15270 个跨越 50 m/s，并新增 282845 个道路观测跨越 30 m/s；M2L 分别减少 290033 个线路 25 m/s 超限和 295291 个道路 30 m/s 超限。所有模式道路 60 m/s 超限数均为 0，因为 a=6 仍固定 55.5。
- 端点映射影响明显大于多项 Step-03D 跨种子波动，例如 `(5,4,0)` 的 M2L/M2H D mean 变化约为 Step-03D seed std 的 `73.46/66.18` 倍，`(4,4,0)` 的 M2H D mean 为 `49.77` 倍。项目没有既有风速映射敏感性验收阈值，本轮未新造门槛。
- 决策为 `INCONCLUSIVE_NEEDS_A6_DATA`：有界等级端点会显著改变 B3 后果，但测试路径实际包含 a=6，且 a=6 缺少可追溯有限上界；因此本轮不正式采用随机风速，也不据此修改固定映射。
- 自动审计 PASS=15、FAIL=0。首次入口在计算前因漏传坐标构建器所需的 `sliceDurationH` 停止，未生成输出；补齐与 Step-03D 相同的 `sliceDurationH=1`、`HresTotalH=3` 后完整运行成功。

### 2026-07-23 - task-002 Step-03F run-001 a=6 最高强度风速数据审计与小规模敏感性

- 当前分支：`task/002-stage2b-b3-smoke`。新增 `audit_a6_historical_wind_data_h2.m`、`evaluate_a6_wind_sensitivity_block_h2.m` 和 `run_stage3f_a6_wind_audit_h2.m`；未修改正式 `a=6 -> 55.5 m/s` 映射、主体样本、候选池、W 转移矩阵、旧 run 或 MSP 文件。
- 仓库来源审计发现 55.5 已存在于初始 Git 快照 `3dd7310...` 的 `fa_h2/fuzhu/terminalLoh_windmc/build_terminal_loh_wind_mc_preview_h2.m`，随后被 Foundation、Persistence 和 Step-03A/B/D 后果链复制使用。定义这些数值的源码附近没有文献、历史数据或配置引用；因此只能确认其仓库内起点为硬编码，无法从 Git 恢复建库前来源。
- CMA 数据使用 1949–2024 共 76 个 `CHYYYYBST.txt` 原始年度文件。官方原始来源为中国气象局热带气旋资料中心 `tcdata.typhoon.org.cn`；因官网 WAF 阻止命令行直接下载，本次通过 ModelScope `ai4s/CMA` 镜像 Commit `19ad9ff02537c962bdaaba9c98f2a5980e251ba9` 获取原文件。RAR SHA-256 为 `1e36a4bf58088a2d9c32fc944c6cba8433a0d735894a6d64a68a5ec4d1aa2105`。
- 风速口径依据中国气象局 2023-08-22 官方答复及 `GB/T 19201-2006`：我国台风强度是台风底层 10 m 高度中心附近最大 2 分钟平均风速。CMA 数据及项目 a=6 阈值均按该 2 分钟口径解释。
- 按 `CMA category=6` 且风速 `>=51 m/s` 筛得 4084 条时间记录、454 个热带气旋，年份范围 1949–2024，75 个年份出现 a=6 记录。记录级 `min/mean/median/q75/q90/q95/q99/max = 52/62.087169/60/65/75/80/90/110 m/s`。历史 110 m/s 仅称为观测样本最大值，不称为物理上界。
- 原文件存在两条分类边界不一致：1 条 `category=6` 但风速 50 m/s，按当前 `>=51` 定义排除；1 条 category=9 变性记录风速 55 m/s，因不是 a=6 排除。筛选规则没有为匹配结果而调整。
- IBTrACS v04r01 仅用于 CMA 数据交叉核查。读取 NOAA/NCEI `IBTrACS.WP.v04r01.nc` 的 `cma_wind`，只保留 CMA agency index=3 且 `iflag=O` 的原始记录；排除 IBTrACS 插值记录、WMO 聚合、USA/JTWC 1 分钟和 JMA/Tokyo 10 分钟风速。NetCDF SHA-256 为 `77b686af554b33ddec7b11d9e32d726ada90c5ce8c4e1a37c2c1d89e39fab5cc`。
- IBTrACS CMA 原始记录同样筛得 4084 条。其 knots 按 `0.514444 m/s` 换算并四舍五入后，与 CMA 4084 条整数 m/s 风速排序样本逐项完全一致，最大差为 0；因此数据口径可比，没有混用不同平均时段。IBTrACS 方法参考 Knapp et al. (2010), BAMS, doi:`10.1175/2009BAMS2755.1`；CMA 数据说明参考 Lu et al. (2021), AAS, doi:`10.1007/s00376-020-0211-7`。
- 配对敏感性沿用 Step-03E 的 5 个代表状态、每状态 2000 条嵌套主体路径和 3 个抗力种子，共 30000 个路径-抗力记录、120000 个模式后果。四模式仅改变 a=6：M0=`55.5`、M6_MEDIAN=`60`、M6_Q90=`75`、M6_Q95=`80 m/s`；a=1:5、路径、线路抗力、道路抗力、径向风场和故障函数完全相同。
- 实际含 a=6 的路径记录为 2495 条，不含 a=6 的为 27505 条；全部不含 a=6 的记录在四模式下 D/A/C、线路和道路后果逐场景完全一致。
- 15 个状态/种子块的跨块平均：M0/Median/Q90/Q95 的 D mean=`153.849821/159.458900/168.676559/169.930475 kg-H2`，全失负荷率=`0.034633/0.041033/0.062700/0.071400`，A=0 share=`0.059838/0.065865/0.093147/0.101821`，reachable-only C mean=`20.035187/20.104458/19.991861/19.762412 km`，W3 故障线路数=`4.485067/4.662667/5.378600/5.610467`，W3 关闭道路数=`2.326967/2.491067/3.246200/3.531267`。
- 只看 2495 条实际含 a=6 的记录，Median/Q90/Q95 相对 M0 的平均 D 增量为 `67.44/178.28/193.35 kg-H2`，全失负荷增量为 `0.08/0.34/0.44`，A=0 share 增量为 `0.07/0.40/0.50`，W3 故障线路增量为 `2.14/10.74/13.53`，W3 关闭道路增量为 `1.97/11.05/14.48`。高风速下 reachable-only C 下降同时伴随 A=0 大幅上升，不解释为更安全。
- Q90/Q95 的主要后果变化超过 Step-03E M0 三种子范围，决策为 `REVISE_A6_MAPPING`。该结论只建议后续单独校准 a=6 代表值；未把 110 m/s 当上界，未自动修改正式 55.5，也未重跑全部 B3。
- 自动审计 PASS=17、FAIL=0，M0 对 Step-03E 最大复现误差 `4.54747350886464e-13`。正式并行计算在结果完整移动后、MATLAB 关闭并行池的进程退出阶段报告 Windows heap corruption，命令返回非零；随后使用独立非并行只读 MATLAB 审计成功退出，确认 60 行模式指标、945 行配对表、4084 条历史记录、全部 17 项 PASS 和关键计数/误差均一致。该退出异常未隐藏。

### 2026-07-24 - task-002 Step-03I run-001 正式逐时随机风速与主体 B3 稳定性

- 当前分支：`task/002-stage2b-b3-smoke`。新增正式配置 `terminalLoh_wdro/config/formal_b3_wind_modes.csv`，将 `stagewise_random_triangular` 设为唯一默认模式，同时完整保留 `fixed_representative`；新增加载器 `load_formal_b3_wind_config_h2.m` 对模式、等级、三角参数、默认标志和 a=6 上限角色进行严格校验。

- 正式风速分布：a1 固定 0；a2～a6 分别为 `Triangular(17.2,20.8,24.4)`、`Triangular(24.5,28.55,32.6)`、`Triangular(32.7,37.05,41.4)`、`Triangular(41.5,46.2,50.9)`、`Triangular(51,55.5,60) m/s`。60 m/s 仅为本研究计算上限，不是官方或物理上限。
- 每条主体记录在 W1/W2/W3 使用三个独立 q，连续同强度时仍重抽。风速随机种子与抗力种子分开管理并可复现，但共同属于第二层 B3 联合后果抽样，不建立第三层 Monte Carlo。
- 线路和道路继续使用 `persistent_fixed_resistance`：每个部件每个记录只生成一次抗力并在 W1～W3 固定；故障、封路和慢行严重度在 3 h 内不恢复。
- 完整计算覆盖 35 个初始状态、每状态 15000 条主体记录、3 个联合种子和 `N=[500,1000,2000,5000,10000,15000]` 嵌套前缀，共完成 1575000 个正式联合场景及 52500 个 N=500 同种子复算场景。105/105 复算一致。
- 主体记录严格按 `1/N` 等权，权重和误差最大 `4.49e-14`，不再次乘 `path_probability`。268 条已观察候选只按主体样本自然出现；外部追加数为 0；858 条未观察候选命中数为 0，仍未进入名义分布。

- 相对同状态同种子 N=15000，N=500/1000/2000/5000/10000 的 p95 绝对误差：D mean=`11.6319/8.2141/5.9531/3.5500/1.8790 kg`；D q95=`56.1874/34.9126/29.1847/19.6383/7.3644 kg`；D q99=`81.5536/45.8228/39.2767/33.0033/9.8192 kg`；全失负荷=`0.0234/0.0192/0.00937/0.00487/0.00277`；A=0=`0.01500/0.00839/0.00584/0.00329/0.00154`；reachable C mean=`0.2417/0.1791/0.1046/0.0570/0.0268 km`。

### 2026-07-24 - task-002 Step-03J run-001 冻结正式 B3 场景集与 WDRO 输入接口

- 当前分支：`task/002-stage2b-b3-smoke`。新增 `evaluate_frozen_wdro_dataset_block_h2.m`、`load_frozen_b3_wdro_dataset_h2.m` 和 `run_stage3j_wdro_input_freeze_h2.m`；未修改 WDRO 距离矩阵、求解算法、MSP、Step-03I 或任何旧 run。
- 读取现有 WDRO reader 后确认旧接口需要 `D(R×33)`、`A(R×4×33)`、`C(R×4×33)`，旧 CSV 通过 `H_node_kg_s/reachable/scenario_service_cost` 每场景展开 132 行。为保持每组 525000 条场景记录，采用场景级 CSV + 精确 DAC MAT sidecar 的双层接口；新 loader 恢复相同 `samples.D/A/C_raw` 数组契约，不修改 WDRO 算法。
- seed group 1=`20260723` 冻结为 nominal；group 2=`20260724` 为 validation-1；group 3=`20260725` 为 validation-2。每组均为 35 状态×每状态 15000 条=`525000` 行，未合并为每状态 45000 条名义样本。
- 三组均按 `initial_state_id + path_id` 规范排序，路径顺序 SHA-256 均为 `a38f2311faeb69b597ec5eec406bce3475113338156884a0c84557e03d0543cf`。每行保留 Step-03I 原始 `joint_stream_position`，因此重排未改变路径对应的随机输入。

- 当前 `DAC_maskedC` 和 LP 实际使用完整节点级 D、完整 A，以及 A=1 位置的 C；路径、风速和种子只用于元数据追踪。loader 读取并返回 `sample_weight`，但求解器签名没有权重参数，且 `solve_wdro_terminal_loh_lp_h2.m` 将 `obj(alpha)` 固定为 `1/R`，因此不支持非等权精确原子。
- 等价键严格使用 `D33 + A132 + Cmasked132` 的原始 double 值，仅把 A=0 或非有限 C 位置按现有掩膜规则置 0；未舍入、未设置近似容差、未按摘要字段分组。每类选择真实原始记录，类权重为 `class_size/15000`，完整 525000 行 path_id 映射保留。
- 35 状态 `K_exact` 最小/均值/中位数/q95/最大为 `116/7100.343/8433/14550.6/14609`。总支撑从 525000 降至 248512，总压缩比例 `0.473356`；只有 7/35 状态不超过 Step-03K 实测 R=1000。最大等价类 14867 条，保留原子中单例类比例 `0.985562`。
- 对全部 3588 个非单例类各检查一对真实成员，现有距离函数组内最大距离为 0；跨类抽查最小距离 `2.258e-8`。低/中/高风险状态的 R=100/250/500 嵌套前缀全部通过零距离映射检查。因求解器不支持非等权，本轮按任务要求只完成能力审计，没有修改核心或运行 Gurobi 对照。

### 2026-07-27 - task-002 Step-03M run-001 WDRO 约束生成小规模精确验证

- 当前分支：`task/002-stage2b-b3-smoke`。新增独立实验程序 `run_stage2_foundation_step03M_wdro_constraint_generation_h2.m` 和 `solve_wdro_terminal_loh_lp_constraint_generation_h2.m`；未修改正式 WDRO 距离矩阵、正式求解器、MSP、Step-03J 冻结数据或旧 run。
- 先审计现有数学形式：目标为 `gamma*sum(T)+rho*lambda+(1/R)*sum(alpha)`；四个 `T` 为 TerminalLOH；`D/A/C` 分别进入现有 `DAC_maskedC` 距离和场景损失；`alpha_r+lambda*d(r,s)>=L_s` 产生恰好 `R^2` 对偶约束。该有限 LP 适合精确 exchange/cutting-surface 分离。
- 从 Step-03J 原始 nominal 数据按 nominal D 均值确定性选择简单/中等/复杂状态 `7/18/30`，测试 `R=100,250,500,1000`、`rho=0,0.02`，共 24 组；未运行 R=15000，未测试全部 35 个状态。
- 新算法从 `(r,r)` 约束开始，每轮对全部 `R^2` 有序场景对分块计算最严重违反并为每个 r 加入一个违反最严重的 s；收敛后再次独立分块扫描全部 `R^2`，未保存完整 R×R 距离矩阵，未使用聚类、抽样、近似最近邻或场景约简。
- 为满足题设 `1e-8` 目标比较，完整旧求解器仍原样调用，仅通过仓库外临时 `gurobi.env` 设置 `OptimalityTol=1e-9`、`FeasibilityTol=1e-9`、`NumericFocus=3`；该设置用于消除已实测默认数值容差造成的 `2.29e-6` alpha/L 目标松弛，不改变旧求解器源码或正式配置。
- 24/24 组均为 `OPTIMAL` 且精确一致：最大目标值绝对误差 `1.455191522836685e-11`，最大四站台 TerminalLOH 绝对误差 `9.947598300641403e-14`，最大收敛后独立全扫描违反量 `1.164153218269348e-10`；自动验收 `PASS=123, FAIL=0`。
- 最终活动约束数占 `R^2` 的比例范围为 `0.001999` 至 `0.0223`，均值 `0.008688083333333334`。约束生成总 Gurobi 求解时间 `13.676935 s`，完整分离及独立复核扫描 `18.3300582 s`，本轮主要耗时为完整分离检查。
- 结论：小规模下与完整 R² 模型精确一致；下一步值得先测试 `R=2000`，`R=5000` 仅在 R=2000 资源和时间结果可接受后继续，不测试 R=15000。
- 本地结果：`terminalLoh_wdro/output/stage2_foundation_step03M_wdro_constraint_generation/`；Git 小型归档：`results/task-002-stage2b-b3-smoke/13-wdro-constraint-generation/run-001/`，8 个文件、28190 字节，逐文件 SHA-256 一致。
- Step-03J nominal CSV/MAT、WDRO 核心文件和 Step-03K 旧结果哈希保持不变；本轮仅运行了明确授权的小规模 WDRO/Gurobi 对比，没有运行正式 WDRO、R=15000、MSP 或其他优化流程。

### 2026-07-27 - task-002 Step-03N run-001 WDRO 约束生成 R=2000 扩展测试

- 基线为 `dbbd04f7c261a018fb248247966e557424b05824`，当前分支为 `task/002-stage2b-b3-smoke`。新增独立 runner `run_step03N_wdro_cg_scale_h2.m`；未修改 Step-03M 求解器、正式 WDRO 核心、MSP、Step-03J 数据或旧 run。
- 继续使用 Step-03M 选定的 nominal 状态 `7/18/30`，每状态前 1000 条做回归、前 2000 条做目标测试，rho=`0,0.02`，共 6 个回归案例和 6 个 R=2000 案例；未运行 R=5000、R=15000 或全部 35 个状态。
- R=2000 完整模型规模为 `334005` 个变量、`4076000` 个约束、`13262000` 个非零元。保守静态峰值估算 `1304.52 MB`，运行前可用 MATLAB 数组内存约 `12076.98 MB`，因此在每案例 300 秒 Gurobi 资源保护下尝试完整 R² 对照。
- 6/6 R=2000 约束生成和完整 R² 案例均为 `OPTIMAL`。最大目标误差 `2.91038304567337e-11`，最大四站 TerminalLOH 误差 `1.09992015495664e-11`，约束生成独立全量扫描最大违反量 `1.16415321826935e-10`，完整模型全量可行性也通过。R=1000 回归 6/6 通过，最大回归目标误差 `2.3283064365387e-10`，最大 TerminalLOH 误差 `3.5527136788005e-14`。
- R=2000 活动约束比例为 `0.00099975–0.00149725`，即 `0.099975%–0.149725%`，平均 `0.1115375%`。R=2000 约束生成平均总时间 `18.4738213 s`，R=1000 平均 `5.34802585 s`，增长 `3.4543x`；目标案例累计 Gurobi 时间 `25.8768017 s`，完整分离及独立复核 `63.8048076 s`，瓶颈仍是完整分离扫描。
- MATLAB 内部结果的最大观测内存约 `2191.85 MB`；运行完整 R² 期间从外部进程观察到的最高私有内存约 `8268.77 MB`、工作集约 `6327.23 MB`，因此后续 R=5000 必须重新做资源估算并单实例、受保护运行。
- 结论：R=2000 结果提供了进入 R=5000 的证据，但只建议在重新估算内存和时间、保持单案例资源保护的条件下进入；不能据此推断 R=15000 可行。R=5000 本轮未运行。
- 本地结果：`terminalLoh_wdro/output/stage2_foundation_step03N_wdro_constraint_generation_scale/run-001/`；Git 归档：`results/task-002-stage2b-b3-smoke/14-wdro-constraint-generation-scale/run-001/`。两处均为 6 个小型审计结果文件及 manifest/summary，归档内容与本地内容一致。
- Step-03J 输入哈希、Step-03M 回归结果哈希和受保护 WDRO 核心哈希保持不变；本轮运行了明确授权的小规模 WDRO/Gurobi 对照，没有运行正式 WDRO、MSP 或其他优化流程。

### 2026-07-27 - task-002 Step-03O run-001 TerminalLOH 结果合理性审计

- 基线为 `96449fa9e66c545b3ea7ba0de3882c57b94297f1`，当前分支为 `task/002-stage2b-b3-smoke`。新增独立审计求解器 `solve_wdro_terminal_loh_audit_cg_h2.m` 和 runner `run_step03O_terminal_loh_audit_h2.m`；未修改正式 WDRO 求解器、Step-03M 约束生成算法、MSP、Step-03J 冻结数据或旧 run。
- 代码审计确认四个 `T_i` 是各站点在每个后果原子内可用于服务的 TerminalLOH 预置储氢/服务量上限。约束为 `sum_n y^s_{i,n} <= T_i`，需求平衡为 `sum_i y^s_{i,n}+u^s_n=D^s_n`；增大 `T_i` 可放宽可服务量并减少缺氢，但同时产生 `gamma*sum(T)` 持有成本。
- 原始储罐容量为 `[300,200,100,150] kg`，正式 WDRO 使用 `capacity_fraction=0.8`，所以 `0 <= T <= [240,160,80,120] kg`。因此多次出现的 `[240,160,80,120]` 正是四站上界，不是偶然的内部解。
- 正式目标为 `gamma*sum(T)+rho*lambda+mean(alpha)`，本轮 `gamma=2`、缺氢罚值 `M=2000`。场景损失由运输服务成本和 `M*sum(u)` 缺氢罚值构成；`D/A/C` 继续按现有 `DAC_maskedC` 距离进入 Wasserstein 对偶约束。
- simple/medium/complex 在 rho=`0/0.02` 的目标值分别为 `767.392774/16840.813159`、`19751.622066/77239.335725`、`186610.352917/330021.692888`。全部目标分解重构误差为 0，Step-03N 端点目标最大复现误差 `8.73115e-10`，TerminalLOH 最大复现误差 `4.68958e-13 kg`。
- medium 的 rho 扫描目标值为 `19751.622066, 25004.376805, 39327.572567, 53623.933885, 77239.335725`，随 rho=`0,0.001,0.005,0.01,0.02` 严格非降。`T` 在 rho<=0.005 时保持全上界；rho=0.01 时为 `[225.9951,145.7722,80,120]`；rho=0.02 时为 `[204.2343,112.0890,69.8002,119.7774]`。complex 在全部扫描半径始终为全上界。
- rho 增大不要求 TerminalLOH 单调增加。rho 改变 `gamma*sum(T)`、`rho*lambda` 与 `mean(alpha)` 的最优权衡；medium 在 rho=0.02 采用较小 `T` 时，总目标更低。将 rho=0 的全上界 `T` 固定到 rho=0.02，目标升至 `77396.790095`，比自由最优值差 `157.454371`；反向固定则比 rho=0 最优值差 `4814.201988`，两组均可行。
- 在 `z*+1e-6` 目标容差下，对 medium 四站分别求最小/最大 `T_i`。rho=0 的区间宽度均低于 `3.7e-8 kg`；rho=0.02 的最大区间宽度为 `3.56888e-5 kg`，站点 2、3 超过本轮 `1e-5 kg` 报告阈值，因此结论标记为 `B. RESULTS_REASONABLE_BUT_MULTIPLE_OPTIMA`。该区间很窄，属于数值近等价最优范围，不是大幅运营歧义。
- 14 项自动验收全部 PASS、FAIL=0；rho 扫描和所有二级/交叉固定求解均做完整独立场景对扫描，最大违反量 `1.16415e-10 <= 1e-8`。Step-03J CSV/MAT、正式 WDRO 核心、Step-03M 约束生成核心和 Step-03N 归档哈希均保持不变。
- 输出：`results/task-002-stage2b-b3-smoke/15-terminal-loh-rationality-audit/run-001/`。本轮只运行三个状态的 R=2000 审计；未运行 R=5000、R=15000、全部 35 状态、MSP、场景约简或聚类。结果支持在重新做资源保护后继续 R=5000 算法规模测试，但边界饱和的 TerminalLOH 必须按容量约束解释，不能据此推断 R=15000 可行。

- 代码审计确认每个灾后样本由完整节点级 `D(33)`、二元可达性 `A(4x33)` 和可达条件下服务距离 `C(4x33)` 表示。正式场景损失只由公共决策 T、D、A、C 以及固定参数决定；路径、风速、种子、线路故障数和道路关闭数只影响上游 D/A/C 或用于追踪，不直接进入 WDRO 损失。
- 当前分量为 `Delta_D=sum|D_r-D_s|`、`Delta_A=sum|A_r-A_s|`、`Delta_C=sum 1(A_r=A_s=1)|C_r-C_s|`，各分量按全样本最大值归一化，基准权重为 `0.6/0.25/0.15`；Step-03P 为可比消融再将每个组合统一到非零中位距离尺度。不可达 C 采用掩膜，不会被当作普通长距离，A 决定能否服务，C 只描述双方均可达时的运输难度。
- D/A/C 平均距离贡献在 simple/medium/complex 分别为 `80.88/7.14/11.98%`、`82.87/10.43/6.70%`、`51.51/42.20/6.28%`。medium/complex 存在 A>0 而 C=0 的独立场景对 `1051/9246`，三状态均存在 C>0 而 A=0 的场景对，且消融会改变 TerminalLOH，因此 A 与 C 不构成不必要重复。
- D 和 A 分量满足抽查的距离公理；pair-dependent masked-C 在每状态 200000 个确定性三元组中出现 `1/6095/42456` 次三角不等式违反，最大违反 `0.9383204058`。虽然当前加权 DAC 样本矩阵未观察到三角违反且无零距离正损失差，函数形式仍不能保证严格度量，因此结论使用“基于自定义 Kantorovich 运输成本的最优运输 DRO”，不称为严格 Wasserstein 距离。
- 33 节点 D、4 服务区 D 和总 D 在 simple 状态给出相同 T，在 medium 状态给出不同 T，complex 状态因四站全容量饱和而相同。节点级 D 保留四站空间布局信息；区域或总量聚合不能作为普遍无损替代，但本轮验证集结果也未证明 D33 在每个状态都数值占优。

- 首次本地 `run-001` 的候选判定逻辑把每个状态都要求出现 A 独立场景对，并在 q95=0 时把零损失并列误计为异常，导致过严结论；该 run 未 Commit/Push、未覆盖。按固定规则保留本地历史并使用新 `run-002`，修正后结论为 `B. DAC_VALID_AS_KANTOROVICH_COST_NOT_STRICT_METRIC`。
- 成功输出：`results/task-002-stage2b-b3-smoke/16-dac-transport-cost-audit/run-002/`。Step-03J 六个大文件 SHA-256 再次全部匹配；未引入车辆调度、站台损坏、场景约简或聚类，未运行 MSP、R=5000 或 R=15000。现有证据支持固定 D/A/C 表征口径后，在单独资源保护下进入 R=5000 约束生成规模测试，但不能据此推断 R=15000 可行。

### 2026-07-27 - task-002 Step-03Q run-001 D + Ctilde 严格距离候选审计

- 基线为 `e6d735446ab47bbc6b379128b653ad96deb3ed1b`，当前分支为 `task/002-stage2b-b3-smoke`。新增独立 `build_step03Q_distance_matrix_h2.m` 和 `run_step03Q_dctilde_strict_metric_audit_h2.m`；显式 method 可选择 old 或 Ctilde，缺省 method 与原距离逐元素完全一致。未修改正式距离函数、正式 WDRO 求解器、MSP、Step-03J、Step-03P run-001/run-002 或其他旧 run。
- 代码链确认 A=1 表示全部需求关键 W1-W3 窗口均存在可服务路径，A=0 表示至少一个关键窗口不可达；C 是当前道路状态下的最短通行阻抗，单位 km。道路关闭将边成本设为 Inf，未关闭道路的 `pClose` 同时进入 `roadLength*(1+pClose)` 减速阻抗，因此 C 同时包含封路绕行和可通行道路减速。
- Ctilde 只取消场景距离中的独立 d_A：双方不可达距离为 0，双方可达距离为 `abs(C_r/C_bound-C_s/C_bound)`，一方不可达距离为 kappa；4×33 个局部距离取平均。A 仍完整保留在供氢可行性上界 `y_i,n^s <= A_i,n^s D_n^s` 中，A=0 继续严格禁止供氢，未从损失模型删除。
- 正式道路边脆弱概率被截断到 `[0,1]`，有限边阻抗不超过 `2*roadLength`；正边权最短路可取不重复边的简单路径。因此严格模型上界为 `C_bound=2*sum(all 41 road lengths)=357.1526447416079 km`，不是 R=2000 样本最大值。nominal/validation-1/validation-2 全部 525000 条的可达 C 最大值为 `129.024752116107/124.252824333777/124.303582800442 km`，超界数均为 0。
- 从正式代码读取旧权重 D/A/C=`0.6/0.25/0.15`，新方案使用 `w_D=0.6`、`w_Ctilde=0.4`。kappa=`0.5/1.0/1.5` 均满足局部距离三角不等式；kappa=0.5 是数学允许的临界值，kappa=1 使可达性切换等于最大归一化连续 C 差异，kappa=1.5 额外强化类别切换，本轮先验主候选为 kappa=1，未使用 validation 反向调参。
- 对 simple/medium/complex 状态 `7/18/30` 的 R=2000 样本，每个新距离执行 200000 个随机三元组检查，并重新生成 Step-03P masked-C 违反三元组 `1/6095/42456` 个做定向检查。Ctilde 和 D+Ctilde 均无超过 `1e-10` 的真实违反，随机最大数值残差 `2.9837e-16`；零距离正损失差为 0。因此候选可作为联合 `(D,Ctilde)` 表征上的严格底层距离。
- 所有 old/new、rho=`0/0.02` 案例均为 OPTIMAL。rho=0 新旧 T/目标最大误差 `1.1369e-13`；rho=0.02 独立全约束扫描最大违反量 `1.164153218269348e-10`。活动约束比例约 `0.1043%-0.1497%`，单案例总调用时间约 `1.71-12.64 s`；记录的 MATLAB 内存快照峰值约 `2.32-2.82 GB`。
- kappa=1 相对旧距离的 TerminalLOH 最大绝对差为 `21.8203638546 kg`。simple 的 validation 综合成本增加约 `7.68-7.73`，medium 降低约 `3442.23-3485.30`，complex 因四站全容量饱和而完全不变；不存在全面、明显恶化。kappa=1.5 在 medium 数值更低，但本轮禁止用 validation 调参，因此不据此改选 kappa。
- 距离与损失差 Spearman 范围为 `0.06156-0.99998`；complex 状态 kappa=1/1.5 将相关性从旧距离 `0.07427` 提高至 `0.20724/0.30922`，小距离大损失异常占比从 `0.12%` 降至 `0.031%/0.017%`。各距离按自身 q05/q95 选择 near/far 对；near 扰动的 T 变化最大 `1.01e-10 kg`，far 最大 `45.7908 kg`，未发现近距离但 TerminalLOH 剧烈变化的反例。
- 自动验收 `PASS=17, FAIL=0`，结论为 `A. CTILDE_STRICT_METRIC_RECOMMENDED`。候选若正式采用，可恢复基于严格底层距离的 1-Wasserstein 表述；但当前正式默认仍是旧 D+A+masked-C。本轮中位数匹配仅用于公平比较，正式替换时必须固定长期尺度并重新校准/验证 rho。
- 输出：`results/task-002-stage2b-b3-smoke/17-dctilde-strict-metric-audit/run-001/`。未运行完整 R2 模型、R=5000、R=15000、全部35状态、MSP、场景约简或聚类；已有未跟踪的 Step-03P 失败历史 run-001 保持原样且不会提交。本轮结果支持在独立资源保护下继续 R=5000 算法规模测试，但不能推断 R=15000 可行。

- 基线为 `45fc03faed0c7a687de6f1198b1ecde01c194fe3`，当前分支为 `task/002-stage2b-b3-smoke`。新增独立单场景 LP 构造器、两阶段确定性 tie-break 求解器、最优范围求解器和 Step-03R runner；未修改正式距离、正式 WDRO 求解器、WDRO 损失、MSP、Step-03J、Step-03Q 或旧 run。
- 使用 Step-03Q 的 simple/medium/complex 状态 `7/18/30`、Step-03J nominal 前 `R=2000`，`d_new=0.6*d_D+0.4*d_Ctilde`、`kappa=1`、完整容量 `[300,200,100,150] kg`。未使用 validation 调参，未运行 R=5000、R=15000 或全部 35 状态。
- 6000 条场景均求解 `gamma*sum(T)+L(T,D_r,A_r,C_r)`，先取得主目标，再在 `obj<=obj_star+1e-8` 下最小化 `T1+2*T2+3*T3+4*T4`。全部主/次级模型和 24000 个站点最优范围均通过；重复求解最大 T/目标误差 `8.2778e-11`，没有场景出现超过 `1e-5 kg` 的多重最优范围。

- `Spearman(d_new,d_T)` 在 simple/medium/complex 为 `0.99999825/0.87597683/0.42021983`；`Spearman(d_new,d_loss_profile)` 为 `0.99999788/0.91929961/0.77086007`。near 的 d_T 中位数范围 `0-0.34819164`，far 为 `0.21899917-0.43376838`；near 损失曲线差中位数范围 `0-491068.98`，far 为 `273300.06-1185391.27`，三个状态均满足 near 中位数严格低于 far。
- 按“距离最低 1% 且 d_T 或损失曲线差最高 1%”定义，三状态均无正式 false-near 对。零距离对最大 `d_T=2.6290e-15`、最大损失曲线差 `2.2737e-13`，满足 `1e-8`。有限反事实共 12 对，覆盖最低距离压力对和 Step-03Q near 扰动对。

- 按全部强制规则保留/选择状态 `7,18,30,31,11,21`：7/18/30 原代表仍覆盖 bulk q75 口径下的主要结构，但状态 31 提供最高 q99.5 固定 T 损失，状态 11 与 7 构成低 bulk 距离集合中 tail95 差异最大的对，状态 21 提供不同 q99.5 受影响站台/节点模式。严格“bulk 最低四分位且 tail 最高四分位”交集为 0，因此不存在更极端的 bulk-close/tail-far 四分位对。
- state 7 的固定 50% 容量损失 q95/q99 阈值为 0，边界零值并列 14872 条；按运行前固定的风险降序、场景编号破并列规则选取 750/150 条，避免把全部零值并列纳入尾部。该集合仍是等质量经验排序集合，不能解释为严格正损失或低概率尾部；q99.5 阈值为 `248.627083900055`。

### 2026-07-29 - task-002 Step-03Z-A run-001 three-period information-structure audit

- Frozen branch, local HEAD, and upstream were `task/002-stage2b-b3-smoke` at `61122dee2cde34736111f0987905da6a3c63d35a`. The audit was static only: solver/Gurobi/WDRO/MSP calls, validation reads, random-scenario generation, and R=2000 reruns were all zero.
- The period SAA has one common four-site `T` vector, scenario-period-specific `y/u`, per-period demand balance, reachability service bounds, and one per-scenario/site capacity row `sum_{tau,n} y <= T_i`. All three periods therefore consume one reserve; there is no period reset, disaster-time replenishment, advance service, shortage carryover, or node inventory.
- All W1-W3 recourse variables are optimized together after the complete scenario arrays are supplied. W1 service can therefore depend on W2/W3 demand, reachability, and cost. No scenario-tree variables, history-shared operating decisions, or nonanticipativity constraints exist. The implemented classification is a two-stage full-scenario-information model with three-period recourse, not a strict multistage sequential-revelation model.
- Period line failures, road closures, and slowdown are deterministically reconstructed with persistent damage logic before deriving `Dperiod/Aperiod/Cperiod`; the formal solver retains period D/A/C but not the intermediate damage arrays. The Step-03Y-F PERIOD path uses those period arrays directly; aggregate D/A/C remain only in the explicit comparator and cross-evaluation paths.
- Existing repository materials define the three one-hour windows, damage persistence, pre-layout purpose, shared reserve, and no advance service/backlog, but do not explicitly state whether all W1-W3 consequences are known before W1 operation or revealed sequentially. Final conclusion: `B. USER_DECISION_REQUIRED_ON_INFORMATION_STRUCTURE`; formal freeze requires an explicit user modeling decision.
- Output: `results/task-002-stage2b-b3-smoke/36-three-period-model-information-audit/run-001/`; mechanical audit `PASS`.

Direct theoretical-probability append is rejected because the selected set is incomplete and selection-biased. ### 2026-08-02 - task-002 Step-04C-C4 run-004 cost provenance and resilience audit

- Frozen branch, local HEAD, upstream, and remote branch were all `task/002-stage2b-b3-smoke` at `62447cb913f5fd3723b5cdf7b3cefccc358bdab2` before the audit. Existing accepted/failed runs, MAT files, complete scenario tables, prepared inputs, checkpoints, historical outputs, and protected MSP files were read-only. No MATLAB, Gurobi, TerminalLOH optimization, formal recourse change, or MSP forward/backward/cut execution occurred.
- Added the deterministic read-only auditor `terminalLoh_wdro/src/run_step04CC4_parameter_resilience_audit.py`. It reads C1 accepted `run-003`, C2 accepted `run-002`, C3 accepted `run-001`, the live `near_stage_msp_input.mat`, and protected C2/C3 scenario tables only to recover shortage CVaR99.5. It creates no scenario/MAT/checkpoint payload.
- The first generated `run-001` passed numerical identities but its provenance table did not enumerate every requested absent/unmodeled item. It is preserved with `FAILED_AUDIT_INCOMPLETE_PARAMETER_COVERAGE`. `run-002` added the missing rows and passed its CSV audits, but its README incorrectly retained the label `Run: run-001`; it is preserved with `FAILED_AUDIT_README_RUN_LABEL`. `run-003` fixed self-identification and passed numerical checks, but the three absent-parameter rows used a generic scan label instead of exact code-path anchors; it is preserved with `FAILED_AUDIT_ABSENT_PARAMETER_SOURCE_PATHS`. `run-004` fixed the provenance anchors before repeating the complete audit without overwriting any failure.
- The effective offline shortage coefficient is `M=2000` from `NearStageInput.Cost.reserve_shortage_penalty_yuan_per_kg`. The MAT field declares `yuan/kg`, but no source was found that calibrates the numeric value to VOLL or an empirical outage cost. The effective preload coefficient is `gamma=0.001*M=2`, documented as a small configurable TerminalLOH holding weight rather than MSP hydrogen-production cost.
- The offline recourse service term is `sum C*y`, where `C` is the current-road-state shortest-path distance in km and `y` is served hydrogen in kg. No `yuan/(kg*km)` multiplier enters this objective. Therefore the complete offline objective mixes kg-km impedance with the monetary-labelled shortage penalty and cannot be treated as a dimensionally pure currency total. In particular, `gamma=2` is not evidenced as `2 yuan/kg`.
- The frozen eta `0.01` decision adds exactly `17.0368707551011 kg` relative to SAA. At the current offline gamma its direct model increment is `34.0737415102021` objective units. This number excludes MSP electricity production, electrolyzer O&M, `0.8 yuan/(kg*km)` HTT transport, `0.05 yuan/kg` ordinary-stage holding, normal-H2 shortage, storage/boil-off loss, and terminal salvage/future value. The last two are not implemented in the audited MSP; external H2 purchase and electric VOLL are also absent.
- Formal C1/C2/C3 demand uses three one-hour periods and `D=outage*P_load_base/(eta_FC*LHV)`, with total load `3715 kW`, `eta_FC=0.55`, and `LHV=33.33 kWh/kg`. The inverse model-equivalent conversion is `18.3315 kWh` per kg of H2 shortage. This supports equivalent unrestored-energy/EENS diagnostics inside the repository convention, but not an independently calibrated monetary EENS or proof that all critical-load weighting is complete. C1 lightweight summaries do not contain shortage CVaR99.5; C2/C3 values were recomputed read-only from their frozen scenario tables.
- Across all 21 C3 distribution-seed cells, both candidates reduce mean operating loss and mean shortage. At gamma `2`, eta `0.003` has mean total cost no higher than SAA in `18/21` cells and eta `0.01` in `15/21`. Nominal gamma break-even ranges are `1.643969-2.613615` for eta `0.003` and `1.403499-1.641199` for eta `0.01`; combined-strong ranges are `14.935213-16.909684` and `11.048791-12.926954`. Complete distribution-by-seed thresholds, including C2, are in `gamma_break_even_by_seed.csv` and the per-distribution min/mean/max table.
- `run-004` contains 24 provenance rows, 84 frozen economic-comparison rows, 56 safety-premium rows, 48 seedwise break-even rows, 16 break-even summaries, and 84 resilience rows. Maximum reconstruction error for `mean_total_cost = 2*sum(T)+mean_operating_loss` is `1.27602106658742e-09`. All frozen C1/C2/C3 certificates remain PASS.
- Accepted output: `results/task-002-stage2b-b3-smoke/49-parameter-provenance-resilience-audit/run-004/`. Bounded conclusion: some physical and MSP monetary parameters are traceable, but the offline objective remains only partly economically interpretable. Retain eta `0.003` as a mild resilience alternative and eta `0.01` as a conservative upper alternative for research comparison, without freezing either or claiming economic superiority. Eta `0.03` divergence was used to numerical tolerance; minimum adversarial probability was `5.77125e-5`, maximum probability/nominal probability ranged from `2.0205` to `6.3723`, and ESS was `14563.1068` in every eta `0.03` case.

It improved q95 shortage in 16/35 states and left 19 unchanged, with no q95 worsening. It improved q99.5 shortage in 28/35 states, left six unchanged, and produced one numerically small worsening. Maximum shortage improved in 17/35 states and was unchanged in 18. Nominal economic cost decreased in 0/35 states; 34 states paid a safety premium and state7 was unchanged.

The Python stage mechanically reconstructs 6053 terminal-hit paths x 4 sites, the MATLAB stage runs only deterministic `eval_h2` on each saved model library with the unchanged frozen OOS table, and the finalizer produces paired flow, constraint, state7 spillover, manifest, and interpretation outputs.

- `run-002` replay used the same OOS file `output_h2/details/h2_OOS.csv`, SHA-256 `6e4ed488423e3cbb838c4a6f8b45019cf4ecf32a82850880399a08aafc7aff85`. SAA/DRO replay elapsed times were `116.9687145/122.0479341 s`. Saved final-inventory error was zero; Step-05B-1 final inventory, ordinary shortage, production, and HTT reproduction errors were at most `5.11591e-13`; terminal-state mismatch count was zero for both policies.
- Across the 24212 terminal-hit path-site rows, positive DRO target increment totals `306571.548997426 kg`. SAA inventory surplus directly covers `286024.557133450 kg`, or `93.297815%`; `83.584521%` of positive-DeltaT rows require no additional inventory. States 1-4 have 100% surplus coverage, zero required extra, and zero remaining gap, so their low Step-05B-2 DeltaI/DeltaT ratios were entirely target-denominator artifacts.
- On positive-DeltaT rows, required extra is `44642.733247757 kg`, realized required extra is `13531.775935937 kg`, and effective realization is `30.311262%`. Across all deficient DRO-target rows, including pre-existing SAA gaps, required extra is `56112.839618933 kg`, realized is `14609.905672768 kg`, and effective realization is `26.036654%`. Thus the earlier low ratio was mostly inflated by unnecessary target increments, but a residual low-realization problem remains.
- A/B/C/D counts are A `21351` (`88.183545%`), B `554` (`2.288122%`), C `1367` (`5.645961%`), and D `940` (`3.882372%`). Within A, 185 rows begin with enough SAA inventory to cover the DRO target but the DRO policy later falls below it, with `541.286430 kg` remaining gap. C contributes `22330.450746439 kg` remaining gap; D contributes `24778.879311687 kg` and has negative aggregate actual-extra.
- State13 surplus coverage is `92.974676%`, required extra `728.779685 kg`, and effective realization `58.476999%`. State14 is `89.378915%`, `2271.091809 kg`, and `65.419116%`. State19 has only `29.612796%` surplus coverage, required extra `2781.807596 kg`, and effective realization `20.849269%`, so state19 remains a genuinely difficult target. Site1 has the largest required extra (`19923.120764 kg`) and remaining gap (`15653.621276 kg`), while site4 has the lowest effective realization (`18.287396%`) and nearly as much remaining gap; site1 is important but not the only bottleneck.
- Eleven C/D state-site pairs were selected by remaining-gap rank plus state13/state14/state19 priorities. Descriptive constraint rates over these selected pair subsets are: electrolyzer binding `94.092040%/96.890547%` for SAA/DRO, tank binding `0.419855%/0.244473%`, aggregate HTT binding `0%/0%`, and ordinary-shortage-positive `36.577529%/37.012852%`. Paths may occur in multiple focal pairs, so these are selected-subset mechanism rates, not global OOS probabilities.
- The dominant active physical ceiling is electrolyzer production capacity. Tank capacity is rarely active and aggregate HTT capacity is never active. The current ordinary LP has no pairwise hard road-reachability constraint; beta affects aggregate HTT capacity and cost. Nevertheless, HTT redistribution matters by site: state14/site1 obtains `+18.978384 kg` final inventory mainly through `+18.427513 kg` net HTT inflow, whereas state13/site2 ends `-3.421108 kg` lower despite `+7.656130 kg` local production because net HTT inflow changes by `-11.077238 kg`.
- Material production and inventory divergence begins at stage 3 for all 11 selected pairs. In 8/11 selected pairs, DRO serves slightly less ordinary demand from stage 4 onward, retaining some reserve while increasing ordinary shortage. Therefore ordinary-demand competition exists, but the missing terminal reserve is not mainly caused by DRO serving more ordinary load.
- State7 is the zero-DeltaT spillover control. Inventory divergence begins at stage 3; DRO adds `27.748811053 kg/path` cumulative production, serves `0.173434879 kg/path` less ordinary load, and ends with `27.922245931 kg/path` more inventory. This confirms multistage policy/cut spillover: TerminalLOH differences in other terminal states affect pre-terminal production and allocation even when the realized terminal state's own target is identical.
- Final mechanism conclusion: current evidence does not justify changing 200 or 2000. The 2000 terminal-gap signal already drives near-continuous production-capacity use and some ordinary-service sacrifice; changing it would not remove the production ceiling and could intensify the service-reserve tradeoff. The next audit should target production capacity and state/site-specific HTT/cut allocation. This step does not declare Pearson DRO successful or unsuccessful.
- Accepted output is `results/task-002-stage2b-b3-smoke/60-terminal-loh-required-extra-and-flow-audit/run-002/`. Independent verification passed for 24212 classification rows, 124 reached state-site aggregates, 704 selected flow rows, 2288 binding rows, 32 state7 paired rows, all formulas, and all replay gates. Core MSP files, `core.md`, and `longtask.md` were not modified. No Commit or Push was performed because the task requires separate explicit authorization.

State19 is physical-total sufficient on `40.9091%`: 26/44 paths are class A and 18/44 are class C, making it primarily a system-total problem with a material policy-allocation minority.
- DRO electrolyzer mean utilization is `95.19%-99.54%` across the seven states, q95 is 100% throughout, and >=99% shares reach `92.52%-98.78%`; production is a credible system-total bottleneck. Storage mean utilization is `44.62%-50.71%`, with >=99% share at most `2.63%`; it is not the dominant limit. Aggregate HTT mean utilization is `10.41%-12.08%`, q95 is `37.25%-47.58%`, maximum is `77.13%`, and >=99% share is zero; HTT capacity is not saturated, although actual spatial allocation remains policy-relevant.
- The current evidence does not justify changing the 200 or 2000 penalties. Class-A paths reflect physical system-total limits under preserved ordinary service; class-C paths should first be examined for cut/value propagation and HTT direction/timing. This audit does not declare Pearson DRO successful or unsuccessful.

- Mechanical source audit confirms Step-05B-4 is an ex-post perfect-information/clairvoyant diagnostic. It selects a realized OOS path and final target, reconstructs the realized ordinary-service matrix, then jointly reoptimizes all earlier physical decisions. It proves feasibility after the full future is known and cannot prove attainability under sequential information.
- The true FA-MSP exogenous information node is the recombining pair `(decision stage t, current Markov state k)`: `forward_pass_h2` chooses `modelLib.models{t,k_t}` and `backward_pass_h2` aggregates with `P_joint(k,:)`; inventory `x` is the endogenous state. All node frequencies use all 10000 frozen OOS paths, and no-terminal descendants remain outcome zero. The audit identifies 289 `(t,k)` nodes, 3221 node-outcome rows, and 1156 node-site dispersion rows. Maximum descendant-probability sum error is `2.0e-15`.
- On physical-feasible but actual-unmet paths, selected counts are state12 `68`, state13 `11`, state14 `42`, and state19 `8`. Exact TerminalLOH-vector certainty remains zero through observed lf=1..6. At lf=6, mean realized-state probabilities are only `5.1926%/4.5893%/5.9208%/8.3756%`, and maximum station-target ranges remain `233.876/215.426/208.252/137.402 kg` for states12/13/14/19. SAA/DRO station inventories begin materially diverging at decision stage 2, before the target vector resolves.
- Positive-target top-station information is more concentrated than the full vector. At latest lf=6 observations, exact top-station certainty shares are `22.2222%/25%/12.5%/100%` for states12/13/14/19. Thus state19's late top-site direction becomes clear, while secondary-station quantities and exact state remain uncertain.
- Existing saved-cut data were sufficient for a limited 12-node read-only value-signal audit. Twenty-four method-node requests produce 96 station rows; active-cut RHS reconstruction error is zero. DRO top-marginal station matches the realized target leader in `3/3` state12, `2/3` state13, `3/3` state14, and `2/3` state19 representative nodes. No solve or cut generation occurred; tied active faces retain min/max/mean marginal coefficients.
- Final bounded classifications are: state12 A information type; state13 A information type; state14 A information type; state19 physical-feasible/actual-unmet subset C mixed type; state16/17/18 and total-insufficient state19 paths E system-total type. State19 merits a targeted late-node cut/training-budget audit, while current evidence does not justify global retraining or changing 200/2000.
- Accepted output is `results/task-002-stage2b-b3-smoke/62-terminal-loh-information-revelation-audit/run-002/`. Maximum LP residual was `5.01821e-14`; the DRO maximum-total and minimum-gap results reproduce Step-05B-4 within `5.68434e-13 kg`. - DRO increases total targets over SAA by `13.092858/16.365232/25.638927/31.546858 kg` for states16/17/18/19. Insufficient shares rise by `1.2821/2.5641/9.0909/13.6364` percentage points, and conditional mean gaps rise by `10.940650/13.302642/15.525819/13.698639 kg`. Across the 255 paired paths, 191 are infeasible under both targets, 50 feasible under both, and 14 SAA-feasible/DRO-infeasible; none transition in the reverse direction. Final system classification is `B`: the SAA gap pre-exists and DRO measurably amplifies system-capacity pressure, especially in states18/19.
- The fixed state19 paths `1529/2134/5371/8092/8289/8650/9095/9913` are all total-sufficient and station-target-feasible in the clairvoyant diagnostic while retaining positive archived DRO terminal gaps; seven of eight actual policy paths already have enough total inventory. This confirms they are allocation/value-signal candidates rather than system-total-shortfall paths, without equating clairvoyant feasibility to non-anticipative attainability.
- Both archived trainings stopped at the one-hour `stop_flag=2`, not formal convergence: SAA `1113` iterations/`1112` completed backward passes and DRO `1199/1198`. Per-iteration sampled `in_sample` paths were not archived, so exact node visit counts and frequencies cannot be reconstructed; frozen-OOS node frequency is kept explicitly separate from training frequency. Every eligible ordinary model has one shared cut per completed backward iteration, which is global cut coverage and not proof of local node visitation.
- At the detailed `path8650, t=5, k=222=(a4,loc7,lf6)` node, frozen-OOS frequency is `33/10000` and the realized state19 descendant probability is `2/33`. DRO site1 has the largest realized target `231.297622 kg` and conditional mean target `237.251710 kg`, but its archived active marginal is lowest: site1/site2/site3/site4 `779.84/1033.28/866/1033.28`. At the fixed archived inventory, the DRO cut envelope last strictly improved at source iteration 397 and did not improve during the next 801 completed backward iterations. SAA shows a related non-target-leading ranking, so the anomaly is not sufficient by itself to establish DRO cut failure.
- Final state19 training classification is `F`: current static cut evidence supports a targeted anomaly candidate but cannot distinguish low local training coverage, insufficient cut approximation, and a legitimate conditional value surface because visit history is absent. If authorized later, the causal test should be a targeted instrumented continuation that archives every sampled `(t,k)` and the fixed-inventory active-cut envelope; current evidence does not justify global retraining from scratch or changing 200/2000.
- Accepted local output is `results/task-002-stage2b-b3-smoke/63-saa-dro-capacity-gap-and-state19-training-audit/run-003/`. The larger gaps therefore come from target growth outrunning reserve growth, not from lower actual inventory.

Feasibility classes are A both feasible `5451`, B SAA feasible/DRO infeasible `175`, C both infeasible `426`, and D SAA infeasible/DRO feasible `1`.
- Three path-level reverse cases are retained: path719/state16, path1318/state25, and path2154/state15. In each, DRO cumulative production is unchanged while ordinary H2 service is lower by about `13.35-18.06 kg`, so more hydrogen can remain at terminal and the maximum feasible inventory rises more than the target. Path2154 is the sole SAA-infeasible/DRO-feasible transition. These are path-specific service/reserve tradeoffs, not lower DRO targets.
- The largest conditional mean gap under both methods is state22 (`109.481880/111.668823 kg` for SAA/DRO), followed by state15 and state33. States16-19 are prominent because persistent shortfalls coincide with materially larger OOS counts and near-full electrolyzer utilization; their targets rank only 15th-19th among all 35 states. Storage and aggregate HTT capacity are not the dominant all-state total-inventory ceiling in this audit.
- Final bounded conclusions remain: the evidence does not justify modifying 200 or 2000, and state19 training sufficiency remains classification F because no new training-history evidence was produced. Further state19 work is optional and should be a targeted instrumented causal test, not another broad static audit.

- Final inventory increases on 5600 paths, is equal within `1e-8 kg` on 4397, and decreases on 3. The top 1%/5%/10% account for `3.814181%/16.018264%/29.487348%` of total positive inventory gain, so the result is neither a uniform small increase nor a rare-tail-only effect.

Mechanism classification is C: non-terminal-hit paths have no realized target check, while terminal-hit input differences are materially compressed by previously audited SAA surplus, production limits, service competition, and sequential information.

- The main research result is negative for the proposed simple risk ordering: 20-50 kg paths have lower mean maximum intensity `2.138` versus `2.364`, lower mean path intensity `2.032` versus `2.182`, lower `a>=5` occurrence `0.026%` versus `3.085%`, lower terminal-hit offline SAA mean EENS `40.993` versus `84.619 kWh`, and lower SAA TerminalLOH `32.478` versus `223.137 kg` than the `<=1 kg` group. These are descriptive associations, not causal effects.
- Terminal-state risk does not map one-to-one to actual inventory response: Spearman rho is `-0.403` for SAA TerminalLOH, `-0.062` for Delta TerminalLOH, `-0.291` for offline mean EENS, `-0.352` for q95, `-0.122` for q99.5, and `+0.208` for maximum shortage. This mixed/weak relation is consistent with broad FA-MSP future-value spillover plus path preparation opportunities and SAA baseline conditions.
- Preparation/production-opportunity count is the strongest independent feature: rho `0.746`. Mean/median Delta I rises from `0/0 kg` at one or two opportunities to `19.273/20.539`, `31.454/35.913`, and `33.749/38.642 kg` at four, five, and six opportunities. `lf=7` is excluded and is not treated as an additional production hour.
- The `<=1 kg` group is not mainly low risk: among its 1642 terminal-hit paths, 1124 (`68.45%`) have offline SAA mean EENS at or above the all-hit median. High SAA surplus is not the dominant whole-group explanation: only 94 (`5.72%`) are at or above the all-hit surplus median, and its mean surplus is `193.539 kg` versus `335.039 kg` for the 20-50 kg group.
- Delta I co-moves very strongly with Delta production (`rho=0.988`), strongly with Delta HTT (`0.666`), very strongly with Delta operating cost (`0.884`), and only very weakly with Delta ordinary shortage (`0.012`, `p=0.248`). HTT remains interpreted only as continuous within-stage resource reconfiguration in the current mathematical model.
- Accepted output is `results/task-002-stage2b-b3-smoke/68-oos-risk-feature-association-audit/run-004/`. Holding the positive-target terminal state fixed, every evaluable state has a negative prep-gap correlation: SAA `18/18` with path-count-weighted mean `-0.521`, DRO `19/19` with `-0.628`. This supports a shared preparation-opportunity dependence in the common FA-MSP implementation layer rather than a Pearson-DRO-specific phenomenon, without alleging model, cut, training, or algorithm error.
- At the same prep counts `2/3/4/5/6`, DRO-minus-SAA mean target is `+68.790/+56.690/+50.111/+48.135/+44.415 kg`; mean actual inventory is `+0.000/+5.764/+18.577/+30.899/+33.366 kg`; mean stationwise gap is `+2.671/+3.680/+2.220/+0.967/-0.063 kg`. Higher DRO targets add raw gap pressure mainly at prep counts 2-5, but this is not evidence of worse DRO implementation capability because actual inventory also rises and the gap difference narrows with more preparation opportunities.
- The formal terminal gap is always `sum_i max(0,T_i-I_i)`. It differs materially from `max(0,sum(T)-sum(I))` on `385` SAA and `501` DRO paths. Ordinary shortage, terminal gap, and W-stage shortage remain distinct, and no `Delta I / Delta T` quantity is described as a realization ratio.

Absolute HTT use falls under both policies. Absolute ordinary shortage and modeled operating cost also fall versus Baseline; within each scheme DRO still has more shortage and a roughly `1301-1312` operating-cost premium over SAA.
- Accepted interpretation is descriptive, not causal. The 11-type statistical-fallacy scan was completed, with explicit protection against aggregate-only interpretation, cost/objective conflation, and causal claims from separately trained stochastic fixed-budget policies.

- Archive sufficiency is explicit: Baseline stores total HTT by path-stage and beta by path-stage, so actual Baseline stage utilization is identifiable. E1 stores cumulative HTT/cost by path but not path-stage HTT. Neither formal archive stores all 12 directed `f_ij` for all 10000 paths. Therefore E1 stage binding and full-OOS directed OD matrices/cost contributions are `DATA_INSUFFICIENT` without policy replay; replay was prohibited and not performed. - E1 cumulative HTT divided by cumulative available capacity is a weaker non-stage certificate: SAA mean/q95/max `2.362%/10.557%/28.474%`, DRO `3.920%/13.655%/29.753%`. It is not labeled as a stage-binding statistic.
- On the common frozen OOS, beta stage4/5/6 means are `0.11996/0.15102/0.18099`, reducing the nominal 160 kg capacity by `19.19/24.16/28.96 kg/stage` to `140.81/135.84/131.04 kg/stage`; the mean transport multipliers are `1.240/1.302/1.362`. The reduction is mechanically material, but Baseline actual use remains far below the resulting effective capacity, so beta capacity reduction is not supported as the principal suppressor.
- Static terminal-check spatial mismatch is common among gap-positive paths but covers a limited share of gap kg under a resource-conserving no-surplus-reuse calculation. Baseline SAA/DRO have `383/501` mismatch paths among `712/900` gap paths (`53.79%/55.67%`) and statically coverable gap shares `7.29%/10.94%`; E1 SAA/DRO have `361/423` among `485/544` (`74.43%/77.76%`) and shares `15.45%/24.97%`. These are static final-inventory diagnostics, not nonanticipative feasible recourse.
- Formal site road nodes are `24/14/18/31`. The shortest-path matrix is preserved, and base transport cost exactly closes to `0.8*d`, spanning `6.4` to `22.5821 yuan/kg`. Actual all-path weighted HTT costs are Baseline SAA/DRO `26.3709/26.2500 yuan/kg` and E1 SAA/DRO `26.0349/26.2876 yuan/kg`. At mean beta, stage4-6 directed prices span `7.935-30.757 yuan/kg`; local production marginal costs vary by stage. HTT pays loaded movement cost only, while source inventory loss remains a separate opportunity value.
- E1 mean production rises relative to Baseline from `293.40` to `328.10 kg/path` for SAA and `308.67` to `354.32 kg/path` for DRO, while mean HTT falls from `32.01` to `13.37 kg/path` and `40.77` to `22.03 kg/path`. This is consistent with stronger local production reducing redistribution need, but is retained as descriptive evidence rather than a causal identification.
- Stage-61 selected-subset directionality has dominant arcs `3->1`, `2->4`, and `2->1`; site3 then site2 are the main exporters and site1 then site4 the main receivers in that subset. It is not generalized to the full 10000 paths.
- `run-001` is preserved after an OOS header-reading error; `run-002` and `run-003` are preserved after installed-Matplotlib compatibility failures; `run-004` and `run-005` were superseded while adding data-sufficiency output and detecting that a stationwise coverage sum could reuse the same surplus; `run-006` corrected the conserving coverage statistic; `run-007` improved figure clarity; accepted `run-008` additionally restores selected-subset OD cost contributions and passes independent verification.
- Independent verification passes with 10000 paths, 6053 terminal hits, maximum utilization error `4.84e-13`, beta-path error `0`, capacity identity error `4.47e-10`, spatial mismatch error `2.20e-8`, base-cost identity error `6.00e-11`, and selected-subset cost error `1.56e-10`. Four figures passed visual inspection.
- Final judgment: HTT inactivity is best explained by the E1 combination of greater local production and lower redistribution need, heterogeneous/limited statically coverable station mismatch, plus transport and inventory-opportunity-value friction. Baseline evidence does not support capacity as the primary limiter. H1 (remove beta capacity reduction while retaining beta cost amplification) is not strongly supported as the next priority; if tested later it should be a narrow secondary diagnostic after instrumented path-stage directed-flow archiving. No H1 experiment was launched.
- Accepted output is `results/task-002-stage2b-b3-smoke/72-htt-behavior-audit/run-008/`. No Commit or Push was authorized or performed.

Analysis failure cannot trigger retraining.
- Per the user's no-accompaniment boundary, after the runner is launched and the first H04 SAA training start is confirmed, Codex stops this turn and does not tail logs, poll, wait, or analyze during solving. All new policies remain explicitly one-hour fixed-budget `stop_flag=2` policies, not formal convergence evidence.

This is descriptive directionality and is not claimed to prove that every transfer is caused by realized TerminalLOH or is nonanticipatively optimal.

Static coverable gap kg also falls, and the remaining gap is less dominated by final surplus-at-another-site mismatch. Static coverage is not treated as feasible nonanticipative recourse.
- More HTT does not substitute for local preparation in these fixed-budget policies. H01 versus Reference raises production by `6.768/8.658 kg/path` and final inventory by `5.936/8.334 kg/path`. Ordinary shortage falls for SAA from `7.066` to `6.234 kg/path` and for DRO from `7.667` to `7.343`; operating cost falls from `11365.69/12669.01` to `11214.22/12445.96 yuan/path`, with H04 SAA as a small non-monotone exception.
- Actual flow-weighted HTT prices are about `26.03-26.29`, `12.51-12.86`, `6.21-6.39`, and `3.07-3.12 yuan/kg` for `c_d=0.8/0.4/0.2/0.1`. At 0.8, stage4 movement exceeds stage4 local production marginal cash cost, while stage5/6 movement remains cheaper before source-inventory opportunity value. The monotone response supports classifying `c_d=0.8` as a material current-model economic friction, not a pure causal estimate.
- Descriptive judgment: activation is already clear at `c_d=0.4`; `c_d=0.2` is the observed middle/elbow candidate with substantial overall and late-stage HTT activation, lower gap/shortage, and lower operating cost. `c_d=0.1` is a stronger low-cost stress case with no observed reciprocal counterflow or frequent capacity saturation. No coefficient is automatically selected as formal, all policies remain fixed-budget/non-converged, and the 11-type statistical-fallacy scan preserves stage/method stratification and causal boundaries.

Site-level differences were heterogeneous, especially higher DRO inventory at sites2/3 and lower at site4.
- The aggregate diagnostic does not establish the proposed causal chain `local grid limitation -> other-site production/inventory -> HTT compensation`: Site3 is locally constrained in some hours but remains a net HTT exporter, and DRO uses less total HTT. Final run-004 judgment is `PASS_READY_FOR_FORMAL_TRAINING`. Results remain local and uncommitted/unpushed as required; no formal training extension or OOS was started.

Typical scales are `0.3715/0.23 p.u.` base P/Q, `0.6 p.u.` branch Smax, `0.02 p.u.` per 200 kW PV, and `0.077 p.u.` total P_EL, with no 1000x discrepancy.

Its SAA/DRO extrema nevertheless equal the independently reconstructible Stage-80 hour21/hour23 hosting extrema. ### 2026-08-16 - task-002 Stage-85N shortage/HTT economic calibration diagnostic

- 重新审计 ordinary shortage 的原因是当前 `200 yuan/kg` 对日常正常用氢未满足的经济权重缺少可恢复的外部来源，且不能与 Stage7 `2000 yuan/kg` TerminalLOH gap penalty 或 W1-W3 `1283.205 yuan/kg-H2` disaster critical-demand value 混用。本轮仅将 `100 yuan/kg` 作为经用户批准的诊断候选，不伪造文献依据，也不冻结为正式参数。
- `c_d` 保持 `0.2 yuan/(kg km)`；新增候选 `c0=5 yuan/kg`，活动小时 HTT 成本严格为 `[5 + 0.2*distance*(1+2*beta)]*f`。`c0` 表示非距离型、按运输质量计的服务/操作成本，不是 fixed dispatch fee、per-trip cost 或 binary activation cost；`f=0` 时成本为0，模型仍为纯连续 LP。
- 所有正式可达 operating OD/beta 的候选单位 HTT 成本 min/median/max 为 `6.6/10.748211643836/18.710558684517 yuan/kg`；100-yuan shortage penalty 分别为其 `15.1515/9.30387/5.34458` 倍。活动 tariff 加 `0.012 yuan/kWh` O&M 对应的直接小时制氢现金系数 min/mean/max 为 `16/33.1367521367521/50.8717948717949 yuan/kg`。
- 四组 paired local-response 诊断复用 Stage-85H CSV 中10条 continuation cuts 和已保存合法库存代理，明确限定为 `LOCAL_RESPONSE_DIAGNOSTIC_ONLY`，不是候选参数下训练出的 policy。12/12 LP 均 OPTIMAL；每个模型 `1129` 个连续变量、`2098` 个不等式、`904` 个等式、integer count `0`、state dimension `4`；48/48 inventory dual 有限，9/9 protected input hashes 不变。
- 三个代表状态合计中，shortage-only 将 `<5/<10 kg` 正流记录从 `11/14` 变为 `8/10`，但 shortage、production 和 total HTT 不变，主要反映局部LP重分配。加入 `c0=5` 才产生主要物理响应：组合候选 total HTT `248.687496942 -> 163.617794476 kg`（减少 `34.2075%`），`<5/<10 kg` 记录 `11/14 -> 2/6`，ordinary shortage 保持 `39.010928138 kg`，且约 `40.86-46.03 kg` 的中大额运输仍存在。
- Final judgment 为 `PASS_STAGE85N_ECONOMIC_CALIBRATION_DIAGNOSTIC`，建议仅为 `PROCEED_TO_FRESH_SHORT_PILOT`。结果身份是 `ECONOMIC_CALIBRATION_DIAGNOSTIC_ONLY / NOT_FORMAL_OOS / NOT_FINAL_PARAMETER_FREEZE`；未训练、OOS、加载checkpoint/modelLib、commit或push，也未自动启动短pilot。

- B->C 并非单纯账面重记：shortage、production、HTT、terminal gap 分别变化 `+3.282965/+2.772240/+10.761130/+3.397138 kg/path`，且 `<10` count 减少 126。但 shortage 和 terminal gap 均明显恶化，objective 增加 `6267.544878 yuan/path`，不能解释为 100 yuan/kg 带来明确额外 policy benefit；建议暂保留 ordinary shortage=200，优先推进 c0=5。
- 三臂每个 operating LP 为 `1129` continuous variables、`2088` base inequalities、`904` equalities、integer count 0；四维 state、LP/dual/cut interface 不变。共 `10662` 个 rollout operating solves 全部有效；最大 equality residual `4.690e-12`、inequality violation `2.328e-10`，inventory/fleet/HTT coefficient/c0 isolation/zero-flow cost/objective decomposition 检查全部 PASS。
- 原始 `run-001` 的三臂训练与 3000-path rollout 完成，但 checkpoint-free finalizer 因 paired merge 列名冲突失败，永久保持 `NEED_FURTHER_DIAGNOSTIC`。`run-002` finalization recovery attempt 又在读取源数据前因 Python 版本不支持 `Path.write_text(newline=...)` 失败并永久保留；`run-003` 完成汇总但自动 judgment 只检查“是否发生实质变化”，未检查 100 元候选的 service-direction benefit，且 README 写死错误 recovery run 号，因此不作为最终接受报告。
- `run-004` 是最终接受的 `FINALIZATION_RECOVERY_ONLY_FROM_RUN001`：未 training、forward/backward、checkpoint load、rollout、OOS 或 optimizer call。26 个 run-001 核心 CSV 在 recovery 前后 bytes/SHA-256 全部一致；最终身份为 `STAGE85O_ECONOMIC_CALIBRATION_FRESH_SHORT_PILOT / COMMON_PATH_DIAGNOSTIC / NOT_FORMAL_OOS / NOT_FINAL_PARAMETER_FREEZE`。
- Final judgment：`PROCEED_WITH_C0_ONLY_CANDIDATE`。未运行正式 OOS、最终长训练、convergence experiment、SAA/DRO final comparison 或 W1-W3 EENS loop；未 commit/push，任务完成后停止等待下一步。

### 2026-08-16 - task-002 Stage-85Q P150 fragmentation mechanism diagnostic

- Pure read-only paired postprocessing used accepted Stage-85O run-004 P100/P200 and Stage-85P run-002 P150 evidence. No MATLAB, Gurobi, checkpoint load, fixed-policy replay, training, forward/backward, cuts, parameter/model change or formal OOS was performed. The identical ordered 1000-path selection retained SHA-256 `e63fefd99306d800e0575696e877a402a25df53dba7ce77f81cca48534b57d4e`; all 10 path/OD/stage/selection source files retained exact bytes and SHA-256 before/after.
- The path-level reconstruction exactly reproduces `<10 kg` counts `1480/2047/1606` and `<5 kg` counts `1109/1562/1322` for P100/P150/P200. `<10` P150 peak paths, defined by `N10_150>N10_100` and `N10_150>N10_200`, number 256 (`25.6%`); `<5` peak paths number 220 (`22.0%`). Peak-path excess is `821/701` `<10` events versus P100/P200 and `672/558` `<5` events, exceeding the global net extras because non-peak paths offset part of the excess.
- Fragmentation is not dominated by a few paths: the top 10/25/50 `<10` peak paths explain only `13.03%/27.77%/43.48%` of peak excess versus P100 and `14.41%/29.53%/46.50%` versus P200. Thus the anomaly is distributed across roughly one quarter of the diagnostic bank, not a handful of paths; all x/1000 remain pressure-selected/stratified `COMMON_PATH_DIAGNOSTIC / NOT_FORMAL_OOS` counts.
- H2 spatial/temporal reconfiguration has descriptive support. On `<10` peak paths, top positive excess versus P200 is Site2->Site4 `215`, Site3->Site1 `136`, and Site2->Site1 `77` events; Site3->Site2 also contributes `77`. Versus P100, Site3->Site1/Site2->Site4/Site3->Site2 contribute `275/203/90`. Stage3/5/4 contribute `227/221/195` versus P200, with hours 17/22/25/32/38 prominent. Top states are mainly moderate-intensity `a=2` states such as k92/k68/k62/k70; aggregated positive excess by intensity is `473/156/65/22` for a=2/3/4/5. P150 positive-flow mean/median is `20.994/10.694 kg`, below P100 `27.184/20.151` and P200 `24.706/17.316`, consistent with lower mass split across more records.
- H1 marginal ordinary-service repair is weakly supported at most. Only `5.28%-8.88%` of positive peak-stage extra events are associated with lower P150 stage shortage; for `<10`, the shares are `6.00%/6.86%` versus P100/P200, while most extra events occur with equal stage shortage. Of 256 `<10` peak paths, 177 have zero shortage under all three policies and only 11 have P150 shortage below both endpoints. Terminal-gap improvement is associated with only `19.35%-25.45%` of peak excess; only 28 peak paths have P150 terminal gap below both. These are associations, not causal flow attribution.
- Existing Stage-85O/P outputs do not save hourly source/destination pre/post inventory, hourly demand/served/shortage, hourly production, I_pre, post-HTT inventory or next-hour pressure. Therefore they cannot prove that a particular 1-10 kg flow avoids a marginal shortage or adjusts terminal reserve. The mechanism-case and stage-detail tables preserve the strongest observable path/stage associations and explicitly mark this field limitation.
- H3 cannot be ruled out because all three policies are independent fresh but limited to 10 iterations and Stage-85Q is prohibited from retraining. The current evidence identifies aggregate spatial/temporal redistribution but neither a sparse path driver nor a complete inventory/service mechanism. Before treating 150-vs-200 fragmentation as a stable parameter response, a separately authorized longer fresh-training robustness comparison is needed. Accepted output is `85Q-p150-fragmentation-mechanism-diagnostic/run-001`; final judgment is `SHORT_TRAINING_ARTIFACT_CANNOT_BE_RULED_OUT`. No commit/push was performed.

### 2026-08-17 - task-002 Stage-85U P200 early-termination path figures

- Mechanically confirmed the lifecycle semantics before selection: ordinary operation requires `a>1` outside `lf=7/8`; `a>1,lf=7` is the Stage7 reserve check; both `a=1` and `lf=8` are absorbing, with `lf=8` the zero-cost boundary. An early path is therefore `terminal_state_id=0` with `operating_stage_count=N` and the next state satisfying `a=1` or `lf=8`; no Stage7 target/gap is assigned.
- Of 10000 P200 OOS paths, 6046 enter Stage7 and 3954 terminate earlier. Early counts after Stage1-6 are `1078/937/919/673/306/41`. Stage3/4/5 split into `a=1` versus `lf=8` absorption as `763/156`, `485/188`, and `215/91` paths.
- Deterministic group-central/mechanism-rich selection retained Stage3 paths `70/2516`, Stage4 paths `3901/1804`, and Stage5 paths `7018/1612`. All six have `terminal_state_id=0`, exact operating horizons `24/32/40 h`, and reserve target/gap status `NOT_APPLICABLE_NO_STAGE7_RESERVE_CHECK`; no post-termination zeros or Figure 5 reserve plot were generated.
- Stage3/4/5 group means are production `334.637514/445.063761/488.145730 kg/path`, ending inventory `360.489951/434.742304/418.475110 kg/path`, HTT `36.032995/47.064770/46.553797 kg/path`, utilization `92.8620%/92.6290%/81.2763%`, and full-load site-hour share `90.2589%/90.2129%/78.4273%`. Production rises with duration, while inventory and HTT are nonmonotonic; no causal claim is made from termination-stage grouping.
- The six selected paths have full-load shares `74.375%-91.406%`; every selected path's last-stage mean system electrolyzer power is below its Stage1 mean by `58.75-693.27 kW`. Path2606 runs 48h with production `648.112541 kg` and ending inventory `611.257945 kg`, above all early-termination group means. This supports treating its long-duration preparation as a difficult continued-evolution feature, while its hourly utilization intensity is not unique because Stage3/4 group means are also high.
- Added `hourly_grid_h2/stage85u_p200_early_termination_figures.py` and backward-compatible optional footer/y-label arguments in `stage85sa_p200_paper_figures.py`. - Across all `a=1` paths, inventory at dissipation is `324.150/308.374 kg` mean/median. This refutes the specific claim that most a=3 paths physically hit the compact system and instead supports tail-risk protection as the main explanation for high a=3 TerminalLOH. Broader physical coverage and severity become material at a=4 and above.

### 2026-08-17 - task-002 Stage-85X TerminalLOH tail-source and Pearson probability diagnostic

- Added the standalone read-only postprocessor `terminalLoh_wdro/src/run_stage85x_terminal_loh_tail_source_diagnostic.py`. Accepted immutable output is `85X-terminal-loh-tail-source-diagnostic/run-003`; it reads Stage-53 accepted run-024, Stage-85W accepted run-007, and the frozen Step-03J W consequence bank only. It performed no W/D/A/C/loss/TerminalLOH recomputation, p-star recovery, inner or outer optimization, MSP/training/rollout, MATLAB, Gurobi, model, eta, q, transition, geometry, or parameter change.
- The full local payload search recorded `13440` matching files. All 70 accepted `case_payload.mat` files passed their `LARGE_FILE_MANIFEST.md` SHA-256 identities and state/eta/T/q/p/loss/objective/certificate gates. The 35 DRO payload `p` fields were mechanically traced to the formal case driver and classified `PERSISTED_FORMAL_P_STAR`, not recovered output. Each p/q vector has 15000 records; all q are exactly 1/15000, and every DRO Pearson divergence is 0.03 within tolerance.
- Formal offline semantics were reconstructed from `run_step04CC6_35state_case.py`. T is a four-site kg vector bounded by `[300,200,100,150]`; total TerminalLOH is its sum. The SAA primary objective is `32.5213675*sum(T) + E_q[1283.205*shortage(T)]`; DRO replaces the nominal expectation by the maximum over `p>=0`, `sum(p)=1`, `sum((p-q)^2/q)<=0.03` on the same fixed support. `C*y` is excluded from the yuan primary objective and Pearson loss and used only as a shortage-preserving lexicographic service-distance tie-break. There is no additional holding/storage/opportunity/salvage or transport monetary term. The main-MSP 2000 yuan/kg Stage7 gap penalty is absent from offline TerminalLOH generation.
- For a=3 loc1-7, SAA total targets are `371.492/382.129/372.310/355.126/325.669/240.570/185.746 kg`; DRO totals are `405.313/405.313/405.313/396.040/382.364/355.126/299.484 kg`. Equal-loc means are `319.006/378.422 kg`, so DRO adds `59.416 kg` or 18.63% relative to the mean SAA target. Maximum uplift is `114.557 kg`; loc6/7 have the largest relative uplifts. The accepted source classification is therefore `MIXED_SAA_HIGH_AND_DRO_UPLIFT`: SAA already creates a high baseline, while Pearson DRO remains a material amplifier.
- At the accepted DRO T, nominal positive-loss mass averages `2.8429%` and worst-case p-star mass averages `4.7314%`; zero-loss mass falls from `97.1571%` to `95.2686%`. Nominal/worst masses are `5.0000%/6.8474%` for the top-5% loss set, `1.0000%/2.4902%` for top-1%, and `0.5000%/1.5436%` for top-0.5%. The 105000-row scenario table preserves q, p-star, delta-p, loss, shortage, formal impact flags, and ranks. Positive-tail shortage reaches `371.346 kg`, while the high T means these are residual fixed-T losses rather than an untreated-disaster distribution.
- The direct preparation cost is `32.521 yuan/kg`; shortage is valued at `1283.205 yuan/kg`, a ratio of about 39.46 to one. Preparation is not free, but there is no additional holding/opportunity cost or salvage term. This asymmetric marginal scale explains why sparse nominal tails can support high SAA T, while p-star reallocation further lifts DRO T. The single 35x4 lookup passed to the MSP compresses the sparse-tail distribution into a reserve-target vector, which is `MECHANISTICALLY_CONSISTENT` with early commitment but is not a causal proof.
- Candidate-T vectors and their per-candidate objective decompositions were not persisted. `a3_terminal_loh_objective_curve.csv` records `OBJECTIVE_CURVE_NOT_PERSISTED`; process LB/UB lines were not misrepresented as a T-grid, and no outer optimization was rerun. The recommended next check is the external basis and completeness of the offline TerminalLOH primary cost structure (`c_H2`, `M_H2/VOLL`, holding/opportunity/salvage), followed by eta; the downstream MSP 2000 gap penalty is a separate question.
- Accepted run-003 contains all required CSVs, README, archived script, and exactly three Chinese PNG figures with one-to-one CSV sources; there are no PDF/SVG files. All three PNGs passed manual inspection and nonblank pixel checks. Audit totals are 88 PASS and one INFO for the unavailable objective curve. Run-001 permanently records a README field-name failure after all scientific gates; run-002 completed but was superseded because its probability-mass figure compressed tail differences and retained English legend labels. Neither was overwritten or deleted.

B therefore remains high relative to the paper at a=2..5, particularly a=3..5. It sets current-stage production to zero, allows 120.12 kg per later active stage, deducts deterministic remaining ordinary demand, and caps the optimistic system bound at 750 kg. Support-wise, 99.9%/99.5% of C2/final-lf7 path-stages have some positive-probability B target that becomes unreachable; production-weighted absolute probability mass is only 3.7%/9.5% (5.7%/11.6% conditional on lf7), so physical catch-up pressure is real but not proved to be the sole or dominant causal driver.
- Primary-source verification found no original-paper procurement-rate cap analogous to 120.12 kg/8h: Assumption 1 and Table 1 state unlimited MDC capacity and SP inventory capacities; equations (1a)-(1g) impose flow balance, inventory, delivery, demand, and sign constraints; Assumption 2 gives a one-period lead time. Equation (32) maps terminal intensity/location to demand, and Sections 4.2-4.3 plus Figures 6-7 document wait-and-see. The comparison attributes the current project's stronger early preparation to finite electrolysis rate, ongoing ordinary demand, and random terminal timing, not to the absent `nu` late-cost mechanism.
- The accepted run contains exactly one core CSV, one concise README, and three visually inspected nonblank PNGs. Independent QA reproduces state65 lf7/a1/lf8 absorption probabilities `0.600377565570/0.355390294361/0.044232140069`, verifies stage production-demand-inventory closure below `4e-11`, decodes all PNGs, and passes `git diff --check` for the postprocessor.

- Static call-chain inspection found a non-whitelisted interface incompatibility before any formal run. `fa_h2/backward_pass_h2.m` adds Stage1 cuts only when `n == params.k_init` (`if t - 1 == 1 && n ~= params.k_init, continue`), and Stage85R trains from one fixed initial state. The final three roles are `BEST_MINIMAL_CANDIDATE=B0001`, `BEST_BALANCED_CANDIDATE=B1011` and `BEST_MAX_GAIN_CANDIDATE=B1111`; `RECOMMEND_NEXT=S4_AND_ASYMMETRIC_SMOKE`, with B1011 as the asymmetric candidate.
